every state of both chains the far-shell nonlinear-balance ratio rho_nl decays outward, so there is no O(1) far-shell rho_nl and no signature of an uncancelled alpha + j term
(which would grow like r^(alpha + 1 - j)); the balance rows at kappa 10 multiply the far-shell rho_nl by shell-dependent factors, 2.86 (on [512, 1024)) to 1.93 (on [16384, 32768))
for omega 0.3 and 5.95 to 3.39 for omega 1 (kappa 10 / kappa 1 per shell; corrected 2026-10-26T12:09Z, lead d128 FY), and steepen its decay: the kappa 10 slopes are close to alpha - 2 = -7/9, which the SPEC 4.4 formula r^(alpha + 1 - j)
gives at j = 3 (the slope an uncancelled twisted term of rate alpha + 3 would give; lead d128 GC); no sampled shell shows the growth r^(alpha + 1 - j), j = 1, 2; the finite sequence
does not identify the source of the residual; the 'decreases outward' statement is a field statement on the table shells [512, 2048) only, the shell [2048, 4096) being partly model
and the three outer shells model (pressure provenance below); whether this is correction structure or residual is not established, and the
|E_ren| reductions of the two chains came with this far-field growth at a flat (omega 1) or 3-4 per cent higher (omega 0.3) core holdout. An interpretation, not a reordering.

  r_last (model beyond it); [4096, 8192), [8192, 16384), [16384, 32768) = MODEL. The far anchor evaluation radii are 1228.8, 4915.2, 19660.8 (evaluation nodes, not source

## Shift 8: Stage III tooling smoke-tested on the CPU (2026-10-26T09:46Z; infrastructure, no GPU use, nothing registered)
- scripts/compare_fields.py implements the SPEC 7.10 field comparison: two saved states (same c_l and omega; L, Nk, channels and R0 may differ) are evaluated on one fresh grid
  (Gauss-Legendre radii in the 7.1 shells from R0_ref 2^-5 to the outermost node of the reference, rotated azimuth, 2 L_ext + 12 x 4 L_ext + 18 angles, plus the three far
  spheres), the second state is rotated about e_3 on its coefficients (exact symmetry) by the angle that minimizes ||V||_* = max over shells of RMS_k(U_ref - U_b) / RMS_k(U_ref),
  and ||V||_*,1 is reported with DU and with Omega; every shell prints RMS of the reference, of the difference and their ratio; the F-gauge int|F|^2 of both states is checked.
  Smoke: a self comparison gives 0; nest6 (0.45, 1) p0 against the S11 weight-1 state of the same branch gives gamma* 0.0051 rad, ||V||_* 5.2e-2, ||V||_*,1 0.115 (DU) / 0.123
  (Omega), with a constant U ratio 4.86e-2 on every shell beyond r = 8 and the far spheres (the leading tail F changed by 4.9 per cent along the ladder) and 6e-3 in the core
  (105 s on 4 CPU threads).
- scripts/reexpand_state.py re-expands a saved state in a basis with a different R0 (or L, Nk, channels) by the linear least-squares fit of transfer.fit_field on the destination
  training grid (tail-constrained coordinates) and writes a start file for init_from; the output is a WARM START, not a reading. Smoke from nest6 (0.45, 1) p0 (R0 0.3, L6 Nk16):
  R0 0.6: relative L2 fit error 2.6e-2 (max pointwise 8.7e-2 at r 0.46), compare_fields ||V||_* 5.3e-2 on the shell [0.25, 0.5), DU 0.20, Omega 0.21, tail beyond r = 16 within
  1.5e-3 (U), F-gauge 0.99978; R0 0.15: fit error 9.0e-3 (max 3.6e-2 at r 1.3), ||V||_* 1.6e-2 on [1, 2), DU 0.098, Omega 0.081, tail 8e-4, F-gauge 1.00014; about 400 s per fit
  on 4 CPU threads. The transfer error concentrates within a factor 4 of the larger of the two R0 values (the Chebyshev nodes in x = (s - R0)/(s + R0) move there); the LM
  re-solve from such a start is the experiment, and its comparison with a same-budget control is made with compare_fields after the solve.
- Referee pass (2026-10-26T10:22Z; astra 'tools_review'): rot_z matches the solver's phase_align on every channel (the coefficient angle gamma realizes U_gamma(y) = R_(-gamma) U(R_gamma y),
  physical angle b = -gamma, now reported); the alignment search now refines the three best coarse basins (an estimated minimum; a single bracket could pick a near-degenerate
  wrong basin); the comparison domains are the worker's holdout shells of the reference plus its far anchor spheres (stated; not the literal SPEC 7.1 r_core / thin-shell
  definition); verdicts of scripts/robustness_reading.py are gated on complete batteries and finite values. Two wording corrections entered in the registration (Appendix B):
  far_radii moves the far anchor residual rows, not an analytic matching boundary (the compactified basis has none), and the X3c criterion is a far-field sensitivity proxy
  (the stability of the fitted pressure-multipole coefficients C_lm of SPEC 7.11 X3 is not measured and stays unevaluated).
- Registered use before the gate (lead d128 EQ, 09:47:39Z): R0 x 2 and x 1/2 re-solves and far-anchor x 1/4 and x 4 re-solves from nest6 (0.45, 1) p0 and nest6 (0.45, 0.3) p0
  at L6 (300 iterations each) and from cont150 (0.45, 1) at L8 (150 iterations), each against a same-budget unchanged control, at queue priority 6 after every registered arm,
  every job ending before 2026-10-27T17:00Z; rule book: reports/STAGE2_ROBUSTNESS_REGISTRATION_20261026.md (+ appendices). Eight L6 jobs enqueued 2026-10-26 (groups (i)-(iii));
  group (iv) follows its degree-8 fits.
- Use: the single-change re-solves of the lead's Stage III plan section 3 (twist radius R0 by a factor 2, matching radius, gauge, shell weights, far-field truncation order) and
  the cross-route comparison of transferred states; nothing launches before the lead's dated registration (lead d128 EL item 7). Files: runs/stage3_smoke/ (log, four JSONs, two
  re-expanded starts).

## S12 radial-lambda rows implemented and the d93 structure reproduced on the training grid (2026-10-25T11:34Z)
- Lead 11:17Z/11:27Z (d93): on the exports a residual component lambda(r) U with lambda > 0 carries 68-79 per cent of int |R_u|^2; this is consistent with the
  negative E_ren under the global identity int U.R_u = beta_0 E_ren, which needs the finite-part and decay hypotheses (a positive sampled pairing is not a sign theorem).
  Reproduced on the primary training grid for nest6 (0.45, 1) p0: radial-lambda share 0.740 (theory route on export samples 0.727), pointwise along-U
  share 0.861 (0.846), lambda max 0.223 at 0.85 R0, lambda > 0 on every octave from 2^-5 to 2^6 R0; the shell-balanced negative radial-average fraction sum_k max(-w_k a_k, 0) / sum_k |w_k a_k| (a_k the
  angular average of U.R_u on node k) is 4e-10, a different quantity from d93's pointwise physical-volume 0.2 per cent. The worker now
  logs this structure (RADIAL LAMBDA line) for every finished start.
- S12 rows (registered before data; R13): sphere rows sqrt(w) sqrt(w_k) sc_k <U.R_u>_k / <|U|^2>_k^(1/2) on every radial training node, with the same
  fixed-gauge scaling and nonlinear shell weight as the momentum rows, so that their squares sum exactly to w times the radial-lambda part of the
  worker's weighted squared momentum residual (test K1 to roundoff; Jacobian 4e-10). They fit the worker's residual without new evaluations (U and R_u at
  the training nodes). Enqueue flag --radial_lambda_w; lines in reports/STAGE2_QUEUE_PREP.md; a 3-iteration smoke job is queued (priority 20).

## S9b at L6: stall on the D6 lineage, destruction on the nest start; both R10 (c) (2026-10-25T12:45Z)

  (E_ren +0.003) but not the helicity balance (H_tot/int|h| -0.19) and destroys the state: holdout 0.448 / 0.447 (9.7x the start), sigma_u^max 7.5, a_max 48,
  carried mismatch 5.8, max E_F 1.25 at 35 R0, max|Omega| 12.9 -> 30. The balances are bought from the far channels and then the core amplitude, which the
  e_core/e_F guards do not pin; the scalar rows at lambda = 1 outweigh the PDE rows by 1e7 in cost. R10 (c); FAILURE_ANALYSIS 31. Proposed Stage II form:
  lambda scaled to the PDE cost (1e-3 to 1e-2, or a continuation from 1e-4) with the core-amplitude pin (S8 row) and closure rows at weight 1 (S11).

## Shift 4 (2026-10-25T10:25Z): SPEC v1.24 7.8 far-helicity coefficients and the far-defect decomposition (all exports)
- Implemented (lead 09:58Z item 1, 09:48Z item 4, 10:12Z; derivation d78, FINAL_REPORT section 2 item 34; the exterior form proved by the verifier's
  flux-ODE argument, d81): src/far_field/correction.py now evaluates on the exact homogeneous tail at r = R0 the leading vorticity coefficient H
  (exact autograd gradient), the leading pressure coefficient Q, K_H(required) = (1 - 3 alpha) Psi_F with Psi_F = int [(F.H) F_r + (Q - |F|^2/2) H_r] dS,
  and K_H(carried) in two forms: the sphere form 2 int G_c.H - (1 - 3 alpha) int n.(F x G_c) (divergence theorem on a shell for the degree-(3 alpha + 1)
  field V_1 x U_0) and the direct form int (F . R0^(2 alpha + 2) curl V_1 + G_c . H) with the exact curl of the carried B/D homogeneous field. Checks on
  every state: the required value by the Psi_F form and by the identity form with G_required agree to 3e-14 (identity (I) of d78 on the computed
  tail), the two carried forms agree to 3e-14, the carried field rebuilt from the combined potentials reproduces G_carried to 6e-16, and the negative
  control (Q + |F|^2/2) differs by 0.36-0.80. The vector defect G_carried - G_required is decomposed into its component along F (the F-component
  int F.(G_carried - G_required) dS = (K_E carried - K_E required)/2, its cosine against F) and its orthogonal norm, both relative to ||G_required||,
  with the d84 bound |K_E ratio - 1| <= m/c. global_balances.integrals takes K_H and adds the second exterior helicity term K_H R^(1 - 3 alpha)/(3 alpha - 1)
  (4e-16 at R_end = 2^20 R0: H_tot and the S9 rows are unchanged to that precision; H_tot_one_term kept). Wired into the worker (FAR HELICITY line and
  ledger fields), reevaluate_holdout.py, export_candidate.py (recomputes the block when it predates v1.24) and the shortlist (three new columns).
  tests/test_far_helicity.py (H1-H4) PASS, in run_tests.sh (23 tests).
- Agreement with the independent route (verifier 09:56Z and 10:15Z, two Cartesian coefficient batches, 544 Decimal80 checks): all eight K_H ratios
  agree to 5e-14 to 4e-12 relative (nest6 (0.45, 1) p0 1.2624652939, p1 1.2122748954; nest6 (0.45, 0.3) p0 4.2415885171, p1 2.1860312087;
  d6w3_L6 1.0740369414; d6w_L6 0.1823254162; nest4 (0.42, 0.3) and S9b 0), the required coefficients to 1e-12, the energy ratios to 1e-12, d6w_L6's
  vector mismatch 1.4190687514 (verifier 1.4190687514) and the perpendicular defect fractions to seven digits (0.5066, 0.4572, 0.5734, 0.3278, 0.4700,
  0.9136 in the same order). The lead's theory-route values (d78) agree to their printed precision. The verifier's degree-8 batch (10:46Z) confirms the two
  L8 rows as well: d6t_L8 K_H ratio 1.390975 (lane 1.3910), energy ratio -2835.41, m 4.4255; d6w_L8 1.598743 (lane 1.5987), -434.48, m 4.2972. Ten of the
  sixteen export rows are now independently confirmed; nest6 (0.42, 0.3) p0 (K_H ratio +1.317, K_E ratio +5.38, m 0.349) is new and primary-only.
- Table for every export: reports/FAR_COEFFICIENTS.md; per export candidates/<id>/meta_supplement_v124.json (meta.json and SHA256.json stay as
  exported; the supplement carries carried_mismatch_rel for d6w_L6 as asked, lead 09:58Z item 3). Readings (approximate states): energy ratio
  K_E(carried)/K_E(required) -32.6 (nest6 (0.45, 1) p0), +29.7 (p1), -8.98 / +19.1 (nest6 (0.45, 0.3) p0 / p1), -3.25 (d6w3_L6), -15.2 (d6w_L6), -434
  (d6w_L8), -2835 (d6t_L8, channels at degree L), 0 for the states without B/D channels (nest4, d6t_L6, S9b); helicity ratio +1.26, +1.21, +4.24, +2.19,
  +1.07, +0.18, +1.60, +1.39, 0. The helicity ratios all have the exact-profile sign and three lie within 0.27 of 1, but the energy ratios of the same
  states are off by factors 3 to 33 with four wrong signs; the vector defect has relative norm m 0.22 (d6w3_L6) to 4.4 (d6t_L8), with 42-67 per cent of
  its square along F (radial modulation of the leading tail) and 33-58 per cent orthogonal (angular shape, same radial order; 91 per cent for d6w_L6).
  No state carries the first nonlinear far correction; a closure row on the F-component alone would fix the energy ratio and leave the angular
  error, so Stage II closure rows stay full vector rows with the F-component and the orthogonal norm recorded separately (lead 10:12Z).
  Primary-reported; the eight K_H ratios, six mismatches and six fractions above are independently confirmed at finite fixed-field scope.

## Shift 3 (2026-10-25T04:51Z): Q_sw, T08 orders 1-2, SPEC v1.21 core sets, S2/S3 prototypes
- Q_sw (lead 04:04Z (a), d50/d55): implemented in src/far_field/ray_functional.py on the dense ray grid (exact azimuthal averages;
  Gamma_0 = <(J y).U>, S = <U_m>.grad Gamma_0 - <(J y).(U.grad U)>, E = <(J y).R_u>; the d50 identity closes to 1e-15 on every state).
  Values agree with the lead's d55 and the verifier's direct coefficient evaluation: nest4_L4 (0.45, 1) Q_sw 2.016 (lead 2.00-2.03, verifier
  2.0009), d6t_L8 0.149 (0.150, 0.1494), d6w3_L6 0.285 (0.285, 0.2848). Every new start logs a SWIRL line and ledgers Q_sw, max|Gamma_0|,
  max|S|, max|E|, closure; the shortlist carries Q_sw next to tau_F and the core eddy energy fraction e_core (lead 04:26Z item 3).
- T08 (gate G1): tau_1, tau_2 of SPEC 7.6 by a degree-4 Taylor fit of the five momentum terms on five spheres eps..5 eps (eps = R0 2^-11) with
  an interleaved second sphere set as the consistency check (src/validation/origin_taylor.py): tests/test_T08_origin_taylor.py PASS (order-0
  coefficient vs the exact R_u(0) 7e-14, DU(0) vs exact 2.6e-9, D^2 U(0) vs central differences 5e-7, fit/check consistency 1e-8). Ledger and
  shortlist carry tau_0/1/2 (floored). The suite (scripts/run_tests.sh) has 20 tests; the clean-process run of 06:04Z gives 19 PASS and the
  strict T05 FAIL (1.35e-12 against 1e-12; see 'Corrections and G1 updates'); reports/G1_TESTS.md is generated from that log.
- SPEC v1.21 (a)-(c) (lead 02:24Z item 2): src/validation/core_resolution.py: a_eff = 2 sqrt2 max|U|/max|Omega| with y_* the located |Omega|
  maximum; the balls B(y_*, a_eff/2), B(y_*, a_eff/4) (sigma_u, a_max with centre and upper weights, sigma_Om on the half ball) and the inner
  dyadic shells to a_eff/16 when the 7.1 core ball exceeds a_eff/16; every added-set RMS recomputed with doubled radial and angular nodes
  ('core RESOLVED' if all changes < 10 per cent). Pressure at the ball points: Chebyshev interpolation of the Green-solution modes P_lm(r)
  and synthesis at the point directions (agrees with the product-grid solver to 1e-12, tests/test_core_resolution.py PASS). Worker CORE line
  and ledger fields (a_eff, sigma_u_ball_half/quarter, a_max_ball_*, sigma_u_max_core, a_max_core, core_resolved); the shortlist's 7.3b
  ordering now also compares sigma_u^max and a_max over all sets and treats core-unresolved or missing states as not ordered. Values for the
  shortlisted states follow from the running re-evaluation (cheap states first; d6w_L8 and d6w3_L8 need a GPU with > 45 GB free).
- S2/S3 prototypes (lead 03:17Z item 1; reports/s2s3_*.json; code src/optimization/backward_defect.py, scripts/s2s3_prototype.py):
  d(U) = U + G N(U) with G in the azimuthal-mode form (O(h^4) cumulative quadrature, 16 radii per octave from R0 2^-14 to R0 2^16, analytic
  cut-off), Galerkin projection P_n onto the tail-constrained basis at the training nodes (fixed-gauge weights), bordered square system with
  the dilation and rotation generators and the two gauge rows.
  (a) Identity G[L U] = U holds to 6.7e-7 (L2) / 3.4e-6 (L4); the two routes for d agree to 2e-6 / 1.5e-5; the forward-mode JVP matches
      central differences to 1e-11 (h 1e-4) and 1e-9 (h 1e-6) at both levels. d is 20 per cent (nest2 L2) / 13 per cent (nest4 L4) of U on the
      dense grid; 35 / 19 per cent of d lies outside the trial space at the training nodes (projection defect, recorded per step).
  (b) One bordered Newton step: L2 (50 unknowns) GMRES needs 28 iterations to 1e-3 (restart 50, no preconditioner), sigma_min 4.5e-3,
      sigma_max 1.0e3 (cond 2.3e5); damped step t = 0.5 lowers ||P_n d|| by 12 per cent and holdout rho_u^max 0.1075 -> 0.1011.
      L4 (242 unknowns): GMRES does NOT reach 1e-3 in 2000 iterations (final 2.9e-2, with or without a Jacobi preconditioner); sigma_min
      8.2e-5, sigma_max 1.0e4 (cond 1.3e8); the full Newton direction is 6.3 times the state, the best damped step (t = 0.125) lowers
      ||P_n d|| by 7 per cent and RAISES holdout rho_u^max 0.0579 -> 0.0625. The Jacobian costs 0.4 s (L2) / 3.4 s (L4) by forward-mode AD
      (dense). L6 (d6t_L6, 674 unknowns): cond 2.5e13 raw, GMRES 1e-2 after 2000 iterations, best damped step t = 1/16 lowers ||P_n d|| by
      4 per cent and raises holdout rho_u^max 0.0552 -> 0.0707. Column-scaled coordinates (basis columns normalized to unit weighted norm, so
      the coefficient scaling is removed; 2026-10-25T05:04Z): cond(J_s) 1.3e3 (L2) / 1.3e5 (L4) / 1.5e7 (L6) against the basis conditioning 23 / 472 /
      8.5e3 at the same levels; GMRES to 1e-3 in the scaled system: 30 iterations (L2), not reached in 2000 (L4: 0.22, L6: 0.11);
      sigma_min(J_s) falls 5.0e-2 -> 1.7e-3 -> 1.1e-4 from L2 to L6 while sigma_max stays 66-1600. Column scaling removes the coefficient
      scaling but not the basis geometry (unit-norm columns are not orthogonal), so these numbers do NOT split the growth between the basis
      and the operator (verifier 05:29Z, finite-matrix proof; an earlier half-and-half attribution here was withdrawn at 05:45Z). The
      field-norm coordinates J_field = diag(R, I) J_s diag(R, I)^(-1) (R the QR factor of the weighted unit-column basis, so coefficient
      perturbations are measured by the weighted field norm; the two border scalars and the two gauge rows keep the Euclidean norm) computed
      2026-10-25T05:46Z: cond(J_field) 1.3e3 (L2; sigma_min 4.8e-2) / 2.5e4 (L4; 4.2e-3) / 3.8e5 (L6; 8.2e-4), unbordered 8.3e2 / 2.7e4 / 4.1e5; GMRES in
      these coordinates: 29 iterations (L2), not converged in 2000 (L4 0.21, L6 0.59). So with the basis geometry removed the projected
      operator's conditioning still grows about 15-20x per level. The matrices (J, J_d, R, cs, gauge rows, border columns, J_s, J_field) are
      saved in reports/s2s3_newton_matrices_<state>.npz for the verifier's audit.
      Reading (per the lead's caveat): compatible with the non-Fredholm/derivative-loss picture of FINAL_REPORT item 24(e) but not
      diagnostic of it; a failure-map entry for II.4. The LM on the shell-weighted least squares does not see this because the Marquardt
      damping regularizes the small singular values.
  (c) Five S3 iterations (stabilized, eta = 2) from the best nest2 L2 state and from the nest4 L4 state, plain normalized control from the
      same starts: every variant leaves the basin at the first step (L2: change 1.68 relative, holdout rho_u 0.108 -> 0.80; L4: change 12.5,
      0.058 -> 0.40; plain control L2 0.108 -> 0.66, L4 0.058 -> 0.24), with erratic successive-change ratios (0.1 to 25) and m(U) changing
      sign by the third iteration. The projected fixed-point map is not contracting near any saved state; <U, T(U)>_eta / <U, U>_eta is
      0.63 (L2) / 0.71 (L4) at the start (an exact profile has 1). Reading: S3 is not usable from Stage I states; S2 needs a preconditioner
      and a much smaller trust region; both are cheap enough to keep as failure-map probes (minutes per step at L4).

## Answer to the lead's viscous-continuation question (04:55Z; written 2026-10-25T04:58Z; nothing started)
- Adding -nu Delta U to the primary residual is cheap. For U = curl(T y) + curl curl(S y), Delta(f y) = (Delta f) y + 2 grad f and curl grad = 0,
  so Delta U = curl((Delta T) y) + curl curl((Delta S) y): the viscous term is the same assembly (Model._assemble) applied to the potentials
  Delta T, Delta S. With T = sum_q T_q(r) P_q(y) (P_q the real solid harmonics, T_q the twisted radial functions) Delta T = sum_q [T_q'' + (2l + 2)
  T_q'/r] P_q, diagonal per (l, m) pair; the twist phi + (omega/(2 c_l)) log(1 + r^2/R0^2) sits inside T_q as the cos/sin pair rotation, so the
  derivatives mix each (m, cos) with its (m, sin) partner through beta'(r), beta''(r) (already handled by Model._twist for the existing
  derivatives). Needed: two more radial derivatives (radial_derivs with K = 6 instead of 4; the Chebyshev radial basis rb.matrices and the
  twist tables take K as an argument), one extra _assemble pass per residual evaluation (about +30 per cent cost; angular tables shared), the
  rows R_u - nu Delta U (pressure unchanged, still -Delta P = tr(DU^2) since div Delta U = 0), AD Jacobian automatic. Estimate: half a shift of
  implementation plus tests (manufactured Delta U against finite differences and against the Cartesian Laplacian of the sampled field).
- Far-field moments in code: TailConstraints (src/optimization/tail_constraints.py) eliminates the rates alpha + 1 and alpha + 2 by two
  linear conditions per A/C column in the second-kind Chebyshev coefficients a_k (x = (s - R0)/(s + R0), s = sqrt(r^2 + R0^2)): sum_k k^2 a_k = 0
  (rate alpha + 1 moment) and (k4 - p).a + q 1.b = 0, -q 1.a + (k4 - p).b = 0 for a cos/sin pair with p = (alpha_env + l)/2, q = m omega/(2 c_l)
  (rate alpha + 2 moment including the twist coupling; k4 = (8/3) k^2 (k^2 - 1)); _solve_pair solves the 4 x 4 system for the top two
  coefficients of the pair given the free ones (rhs = minus the moments of the free coefficients). With nu > 0 the rate alpha + 2 balance
  L[U_2] = nu Delta U_0 has L = -2 c_l on that rate (eigenvalue (1 - c_l) - c_l (alpha + j) = -j c_l), so U_2 = -(nu / (2 c_l)) Delta_twisted U_0
  is determined by the leading coefficients: the alpha + 2 moment condition becomes (k4 - p).a + q 1.b = nu M_lm(a_lead, b_lead) with M_lm a
  fixed linear map of the leading amplitudes (sum_k a_k etc.), i.e. still a linear elimination (rhs gains the nu term), parameterized by nu.
  The alpha + 1 condition stays homogeneous (no viscous source at that rate). Both changes are local to TailConstraints._solve_pair /
  _solve_single and the leading-tail amplitude formulas (Problem.tail_amplitudes). Nothing is started before the S8 registration.
